ICHALKARANJI MUNICIPAL COUNCIL, ICHALKARANJI
Maharashtra Municipal Corporation Act, 1949
NOTIFICATION
No. -/../-.--In exercise of the powers conferred by Section 5 (3) of The
Maharashtra Municipal Corporation Act, (Bom. LIX of 1949) (hereinafter referred to as the “said Act” )
after carefully taking into consideration the objections and suggestions received, in response to the
Draft Notification No. 12, dated 4th September 2025 fixing for the Ichalkaranji Municipal Corporation.
The Number and the extent of the Wards into which the said Municipal Area shall be divided is shown
in the schedule to said Draft Notification. The State Government or its Authorised Officer, with the
approval of the State Election Commission, Maharashtra or its Authorised Officer, hereby makes the
following Notification ;
whereas the State Government or its Authorised Officer with the approval of the State Election
Commission, Maharashtra or its Authorised Officer, as specified for Ichalkaranji Municipal Corporation
under Section 5 of the Maharashtra Municipal Corporation Act, the Number of Councillors to be elected
and number of Wards is as follows :-
1. Total number of Councillors - 65
2. Four Councillors Wards - 15
3. Five Councillors Wards - 01
4. Total Wards - 16
Now, therefore, in exercise of the powers under Section 5 (3) of the Maharashtra Municipal
Corporation Act, the State Government or its Authorised Officer with the approval of the State Election
Commission, Maharashtra or its Authorised Officer hereby fix the number and extent of the Wards into
which the said Municipal Corporation Area shall be divided as shown in the schedule to this
Notification.
This Notification shall take effect for the purpose of the next General Election held immediately
following the date of this Notification.

NOTIFICATION
No. SEC/MNC-2025/26/KA-5/2025.-- In exercise of the powers conferred by Section 5 (3) of
The Maharashtra Municipal Corporation Act, (Bom. LIX of 1949) (hereinafter referred to as the "said
Act") after carefully taking into consideration the objections and suggestions received, in response to
the Draft Notification No. 11, Government of Maharashtra Gazette, Extra-ordinary Part-1-A--Pune
Divisional Supplement, 3rd September 2025/Bhadra-12, Shake 1947, Kolhapur dated 03rd September
2025 fixing for the Kolhapur Municipal Corporation. The number and the extent of the Wards into which
the said Municipal Area shall be divided is shown in the schedule to said Draft Notification. The State
Government or its Authorised Officer, with the approval of the State Election Commission, Maharashtra
or its Authorised Officer, hereby makes the following Notification.
whereas the State Government or its Authorised Officer with the approval of the State Election
Commission, Maharashtra or its Authorised Officer, as specified for Kolhapur Municipal Corporation
under Section 5 of the Maharashtra Municipal Corporation Act, the Number of Councillors to be elected
and number of Wards is as follows:-
1. Total number of Councillors -- 81
2. Four Councillors Wards -- 19
3. Five Councillors Wards -- 01
4. Total Wards -- 20
Now, therefore, in exercise of the powers under Section 5 (3) of the Maharashtra Municipal
Corporation Act, The State Government or its authorised officer with the approval of the State Election
Commission, Maharashtra or its authorised officer hereby fix the number and extent of the Wards into
which the said Municipal Corporation area shall be divided as shown in the schedule to this
Notification.
This Notification shall take effect for the purpose of the next General Election held immediately
following the date of this Notification.

SANGLI-MIRAJ AND KUPWAD CITY MUNICIPAL COUNCIL, SANGLI
Maharashtra Municipal Corporation Act, 1949
NOTIFICATION
No. SEC/MNC-2025/2/KA-5/2025.-- In exercise of the powers conferred by Section 5 (3) of
The Maharashtra Municipal Corporation Act, (Bom. LIX of 1949) (hereinafter referred to as the "said
Act") after carefully taking into consideration the objections and suggestions received, in response to
the Draft Notification No.dated 3rd September 2025 fixing for the Sangli-Miraj and Kupwad
Municipal Corporation. The number and the extent of the Wards into which the said Municipal Area
shall be divided is shown in the schedule to said Draft Notification. The State Government or its
Authorised Officer, with the approval of the State Election Commission, Maharashtra or its Authorised
Officer, hereby makes the following Notification.
whereas the State Government or its Authorised Officer with the approval of the State Election
Commission, Maharashtra or its Authorised Officer, as specified for Sangli-Miraj and Kupwad Municipal
Corporation under Section 5 of the Maharashtra Municipal Corporation Act, the Number of Councillors
to be elected and number of Wards is as follows:-
1. Total number of Councillors -- 78
2. Four Councillors Wards -- 18
3. Three Councillors Wards -- 02
4. Total Wards -- 20
Now, therefore, in exercise of the powers under Section 5 (3) of the Maharashtra Municipal
Corporation Act, The State Government or its Authorised Officer with the approval of the State Election
Commission, Maharashtra or its Authorised Officer hereby fix the number and extent of the Wards into
which the said Municipal Corporation area shall be divided as shown in the schedule to this
Notification.
This Notification shall take effect for the purpose of the next General Election held immediately
following the date of this Notification.

BARAMATI MUNICIPAL COUNCIL, BARAMATI
No: PSN/KV/KARYA-01/1836/2025.--- Hon. State Election Commission : Order No. RANIA/NP-
2025/Pr.No.10/Ka-6, dated 03/10/2025, and LetterNo. RANIA/NP-2025/Pr.No.11/Ka-6, dated
03/10/2025
Whereas, as per the schedule issued by the Urban Development Department dated
10/06/2025, the final geographical boundaries of wards for the upcoming elections of the Baramati
Municipal Council have been published in the Government of Maharashtra Gazette, Extraordinary Part
One-A – Pune Divisional Supplement, dated 29th September 2025.
And whereas, as per the orders of the Hon. State Election Commission, Order No. RANIA/NP-
2025/Pr.No.10/Ka-6, dated 03/10/2025, and Order No. RANIA/NP-2025/Pr.No.11/Ka-6, dated
03/10/2025, the powers to publish a Draft Notification for reservation draw have been delegated.
Accordingly, as per the schedule mentioned in the said orders, the reservation draw for the Wards of
the Baramati Municipal Council was conducted and published on 08th October 2025.
Therefore, I, Jitendra Dudi, District Collector, Pune, in exercise of the powers vested in me
under the above-mentioned orders of the Hon. State Election Commission and as per the order of the
Urban Development Department dated 10/06/2025, hereby publish the Draft Proposal of Reservation
for the upcoming General Elections in the election wards of the Baramati Municipal Council , as notified
in the Government of Maharashtra Gazette, Extraordinary Part One-A – Pune Divisional Supplement,
dated 29th September 2025.
In accordance with this notification, if citizens have any objections or suggestions, they should
submit them to the Chief Officer, Baramati Municipal Council on or before 14th October 2025.
Objections or suggestions received after this date will not be considered.

FURSUNGI-URULI DAVACH MUNICIPAL COUNCIL, FURSUNGI
No: PSN/KV/KARYA-01/1836/2025.--- Hon. State Election Commission : Order No. RANIA/NP-
2025/Pr.No.10/Ka-6, dated 03/10/2025, and LetterNo. RANIA/NP-2025/Pr.No.11/Ka-6, dated
03/10/2025
Whereas, as per the schedule issued by the Urban Development Department dated
10/06/2025, the final geographical boundaries of wards for the upcoming elections of the Fursungi-
Uruli Devach Municipal Council have been published in the Government of Maharashtra Gazette,
Extraordinary Part One-A – Pune Divisional Supplement, dated 29th September 2025.
And whereas, as per the orders of the Hon. State Election Commission, Order No. RANIA/NP-
2025/Pr.No.10/Ka-6, dated 03/10/2025, and Order No. RANIA/NP-2025/Pr.No.11/Ka-6, dated
03/10/2025, the powers to publish a Draft Notification for reservation draw have been delegated.
Accordingly, as per the schedule mentioned in the said orders, the reservation draw for the Wards of
the Fursungi-Uruli Devach Municipal Council was conducted and published on 08th October 2025.
Therefore, I, Jitendra Dudi, District Collector, Pune, in exercise of the powers vested in me
under the above-mentioned orders of the Hon. State Election Commission and as per the order of the
Urban Development Department dated 10/06/2025, hereby publish the Draft Proposal of Reservation
for the upcoming General Elections in the election wards of the Fursungi-Uruli Devach Municipal
Council , as notified in the Government of Maharashtra Gazette, Extraordinary Part One-A – Pune
Divisional Supplement, dated 29th September 2025.
In accordance with this notification, if citizens have any objections or suggestions, they should
submit them to the Chief Officer, Baramati Municipal Council on or before 14th October 2025.
Objections or suggestions received after this date will not be considered.

LONAVALA MUNICIPAL COUNCIL, LONAVALA
No. PSN/KV/KARYA-01/ 1836/2025.—Hon. State Election Commission Order No. RANIA/NP-
2025/PR.No.10 (A)/KA-6, dated 03/10/2025.
Whereas, as per the order issued by the Urban Development Department dated 10/06/2025,
the number and extent of the Wards into which the area of Lonavala Municipal Council shall be
divided for the purpose of the next General Election have been published in the Official Gazette vide
notification dated 29th September 2025, Maharashtra Government Gazette, Extraordinary Part 1-A
Pune Divisional Supplement.
And whereas, the Hon'ble State Election Commission vide order No- RANIA/NP-
2025/PR.No.10/KA-6, dated 03/10/2025 has empowered to draw the reservation and publish the draft
notification and as per the programme given under the said order, the draw of reserved wards of
Lonavala Municipality has been drawn on 08th October 2025.
Now therefore I, Jitendra Dudi, District Collector, Pune, in exercise of the powers confered by
Hon'ble State Election Commission’s aforesaid order, hereby notify the draft of following reservation of
seats for the purpose of the next general election in the electoral wards published in the Official
Gazette in respect of Lonavala Municipality vide notification Maharashtra Government Gazette,
Extraordinary Part 1-A Pune Divisional Supplement, dated 29th September, 2025, as directed by the
Urban Development Department order dated 10/06/2025. Any objections or suggestions of the citizens
in pursuance of the said notification should be submitted to the Chief Officer, Lonavala Municipal
Council on or before 14/10/2025. Objections and suggestions received after the said date will not be
considered.

2025/Pr.No.10/Ka-6, dated 03/10/2025, and Order No. RANIA/NP-2025/Pr.No.11/Ka-6, dated
03/10/2025, the powers to publish a Draft Notification for reservation draw have been delegated.
Accordingly, as per the schedule mentioned in the said orders, the reservation draw for the Wards of
the Chakan Municipal Council was conducted and published on 08th October 2025.
Therefore, I, Jitendra Dudi, District Collector, Pune, in exercise of the powers vested in me
under the above-mentioned orders of the Hon. State Election Commission and as per the order of the
Urban Development Department dated 10/06/2025, hereby publish the Draft Proposal of Reservation
for the upcoming General Elections in the election wards of the Chakan Municipal Council , as notified
in the Government of Maharashtra Gazette, Extraordinary Part One-A – Pune Divisional Supplement,
dated 29th September 2025.
In accordance with this notification, if citizens have any objections or suggestions, they should
submit them to the Chief Officer, Dr. Ankush Jadhav, Chakan Municipal Council on or before 14th
October 2025. Objections or suggestions received after this date will not be considered.

JEJURI DAVACH MUNICIPAL COUNCIL, JEJURI
No: PSN/KV/KARYA-01/1836/2025.--- Hon. State Election Commission : Order No. RANIA/NP-
2025/Pr.No.10/Ka-6, dated 03/10/2025, and LetterNo. RANIA/NP-2025/Pr.No.11/Ka-6, dated
03/10/2025
Whereas, as per the schedule issued by the Urban Development Department dated
10/06/2025, the final geographical boundaries of wards for the upcoming elections of the Jejuri
Municipal Council have been published in the Government of Maharashtra Gazette, Extraordinary Part
One-A – Pune Divisional Supplement, dated 29th September 2025.
And whereas, as per the orders of the Hon. State Election Commission, Order No. RANIA/NP-
2025/Pr.No.10/Ka-6, dated 03/10/2025, and Order No. RANIA/NP-2025/Pr.No.11/Ka-6, dated
03/10/2025, the powers to publish a Draft Notification for reservation draw have been delegated.
Accordingly, as per the schedule mentioned in the said orders, the reservation draw for the Wards of
the Jejuri Municipal Council was conducted and published on 08th October 2025.
Therefore, I, Jitendra Dudi, District Collector, Pune, in exercise of the powers vested in me
under the above-mentioned orders of the Hon. State Election Commission and as per the order of the
Urban Development Department dated 10/06/2025, hereby publish the Draft Proposal of Reservation
for the upcoming General Elections in the election wards of the Jejuri Municipal Council , as notified in
the Government of Maharashtra Gazette, Extraordinary Part One-A – Pune Divisional Supplement,
dated 29th September 2025.
In accordance with this notification, if citizens have any objections or suggestions, they should
submit them to the Chief Officer, Jejuri Municipal Council on or before 14th October 2025. Objections
or suggestions received after this date will not be considered.

BHOR MUNICIPAL COUNCIL, BHOR
No: PSN/KV/KARYA-01/1836/2025.--- Hon. State Election Commission : Order No. RANIA/NP-
2025/Pr.No.10/Ka-6, dated 03/10/2025, and LetterNo. RANIA/NP-2025/Pr.No.11/Ka-6, dated
03/10/2025
Whereas, as per the schedule issued by the Urban Development Department dated
10/06/2025, the final geographical boundaries of wards for the upcoming elections of the Bhor
Municipal Council have been published in the Government of Maharashtra Gazette, Extraordinary Part
One-A – Pune Divisional Supplement, dated 29th September 2025.
And whereas, as per the orders of the Hon. State Election Commission, Order No. RANIA/NP-
2025/Pr.No.10/Ka-6, dated 03/10/2025, and Order No. RANIA/NP-2025/Pr.No.11/Ka-6, dated
03/10/2025, the powers to publish a Draft Notification for reservation draw have been delegated.
Accordingly, as per the schedule mentioned in the said orders, the reservation draw for the Wards of
the Bhor Municipal Council was conducted and published on 08th October 2025.
Therefore, I, Jitendra Dudi, District Collector, Pune, in exercise of the powers vested in me
under the above-mentioned orders of the Hon. State Election Commission and as per the order of the
Urban Development Department dated 10/06/2025, hereby publish the Draft Proposal of Reservation
for the upcoming General Elections in the election wards of the Bhor Municipal Council , as notified in
the Government of Maharashtra Gazette, Extraordinary Part One-A – Pune Divisional Supplement,
dated 29th September 2025.
In accordance with this notification, if citizens have any objections or suggestions, they should
submit them to the Chief Officer, Bhor Municipal Council on or before 14th October 2025. Objections or
suggestions received after this date will not be considered.

SHIRUR MUNICIPAL COUNCIL, SHIRUR
No: PSN/KV/KARYA-01/1836/2025.--Hon.State Election Commission:Order No.RANIA/NP-
2025/Pr.No.10/Ka-6,dated 03/10/2025 and LetterNo. RANIA/NP-2025/Pr.No.11/Ka-6, dated
03/10/2025
Whereas, as per the schedule issued by the Urban Development Department dated
10/06/2025, the final geographical boundaries of wards for the upcoming elections of the Shirur
Municipal Council have been published in the Government of Maharashtra Gazette, Extraordinary Part
One-A – Pune Divisional Supplement, dated 29th September 2025 ;
And whereas, as per the orders of the Hon.State Election Commission, Order No. RANIA/NP-
2025/Pr.No.10/Ka-6,dated 03/10/2025, and Order No. RANIA/NP-2025/Pr.No.11/Ka-6, dated
03/10/2025, the powers to publish a draft notification for reservation draw have been delegated.
Accordingly, as per the schedule mentioned in the said orders, the reservation draw for the wards of
the Shirur Municipal Council was conducted and published on 08th October 2025.
Therefore, I, Jitendra Dudi, District Collector, Pune, in exercise of the powers vested in me
under the above-mentioned orders of the Hon. State Election Commission and as per the order of the
Urban Development Department dated 10/06/2025, hereby publish the draft proposal of reservation for
the upcoming general elections in the election wards of the Shirur Municipal Council, as notified in the
Government of Maharashtra Gazette, Extraordinary Part One-A – Pune Divisional Supplement, dated
29th September 2025.
In accordance with this notification, if citizens have any objections or suggestions, they should
submit them to the Chief Officer, Shirur Municipal Council on or before 14th October 2025. Objections
or suggestions received after this date will not be considered.

03/10/2025
Whereas, as per the schedule issued by the Urban Development Department dated
10/06/2025, the final geographical boundaries of wards for the upcoming elections of the Alandi
Municipal Council have been published in the Government of Maharashtra Gazette, Extraordinary Part
One-A – Pune Divisional Supplement, dated 29th September 2025.
And whereas, as per the orders of the Hon. State Election Commission, Order No. RANIA/NP-
2025/Pr.No.10/Ka-6, dated 03/10/2025, and Order No. RANIA/NP-2025/Pr.No.11/Ka-6, dated
03/10/2025, the powers to publish a draft notification for reservation draw have been delegated.
Accordingly, as per the schedule mentioned in the said orders, the reservation draw for the wards of
the Alandi Municipal Council was conducted and published on 08th October 2025.
Therefore, I, Jitendra Dudi, District Collector, Pune, in exercise of the powers vested in me

or suggestions received after this date will not be considered.

VADGAON NAGARPANCHAYAT, VADGAON
No. PSN/KV/KARYA-01/1836//2025.--Hon. State Election Commission: Order No.
RANIA/NP-2025/Pr.No.10/Ka-6, dated 03/10/2025 and Letter No. RANIA/NP-2025/ Pr.No.11/Ka-6,
dated 03/10/2025
Whereas, as per the schedule issued by the Urban Development Department dated
10/06/2025, the final geographical boundaries of wards for the upcoming elections of the Vadgaon
Nagarpanchayat have been published in the Government of Maharashtra Gazette, Extraordinary Part
One-A – Pune Divisional Supplement, dated 29th September 2025.
And whereas, as per the orders of the Hon. State Election Commission, Order No. RANIA/NP-
2025/Pr.No.10/Ka-6, dated 03/10/2025, and Order No. RANIA/NP-2025/Pr.No.11/Ka-6, dated
03/10/2025, the powers to publish a draft notification for reservation draw have been delegated.
Accordingly, as per the schedule mentioned in the said orders, the reservation draw for the wards of
the Vadgaon Nagarpanchayat was conducted and published on 08th October 2025.
Therefore, I, Jitendra Dudi, District Collector, Pune, in exercise of the powers vested in me
under the above-mentioned orders of the Hon. State Election Commission and as per the order of the
Urban Development Department dated 10/06/2025, hereby publish the draft proposal of reservation for
the upcoming general elections in the election wards of the Vadgaon Nagarpanchayat, as notified in
the Government of Maharashtra Gazette, Extraordinary Part One-A – Pune Divisional Supplement,
dated 29th September 2025.
In accordance with this notification, if citizens have any objections or suggestions, they should
submit them to the Chief Officer, Vadgaon Nagarpanchayat in writing on or before 14th October 2025.
Objections or suggestions received after this date will not be considered.

MALEGAON BK. NAGARPANCHAYAT, MALEBGAON
No: PSN/KV/KARYA-01/1836/2025.--Hon. State Election Commission Order No. RANIA/NP-
2025/Pr.No.10(B)/Ka-6, dated 03/10/2025, and Letter No. RANIA/NP-2025/Pr.No.11/Ka-6, dated
03/10/2025.

Whereas, as per the schedule issued by the Urban Development Department dated
10/06/2025, the final geographical boundaries of wards for the upcoming elections of the Malegaon Bk.
Nagarpanchayat have been published in the Government of Maharashtra Gazette, Extraordinary Part
One-A – Pune Divisional Supplement, dated 29th September 2025.
And whereas, as per the orders of the Hon. State Election Commission, Order No. RANIA/NP-
2025/Pr.No.10(B)/Ka-6, dated 03/10/2025, and Order No. RANIA/NP-2025/Pr.No.11/Ka-6,
dated 03/10/2025, the powers to publish a draft notification for reservation draw have been
delegated. Accordingly, as per the schedule mentioned in the said orders, the reservation draw for the
wards of the Malegaon Bk Nagarpanchayat was conducted and published on 08th October 2025.
Therefore, I, Jitendra Dudi, District Collector, Pune, in exercise of the powers vested in me
under the above-mentioned orders of the Hon. State Election Commission and as per the order of the
Urban Development Department dated 10/06/2025, hereby publish the draft proposal of reservation for
the upcoming general elections in the election wards of the Malegaon Bk. Nagarpanchayat, as notified
in the Government of Maharashtra Gazette, Extraordinary Part One-A – Pune Divisional Supplement,
dated 29th September 2025.
In accordance with this notification, if citizens have any objections or suggestions, they should
submit them to the Chief Officer, Malegaon Bk. Nagarpanchayat on or before 14th October 2025.
Objections or suggestions received after this date will not be considered.

MANCHAR NAGARPANCHAYAT, MANCHAR
No: PSN/KV/KARYA-01/ 1836/2025.--Hon. State Election Commission: Order No.RANIA/NP-
2025/Pr.No.10/Ka-6, dated 03/10/2025, and Letter No. RANIA/NP-2025/Pr.No.11/Ka-6, dated
03/10/2025
Whereas, as per the schedule issued by the Urban Development Department dated
10/06/2025, the final geographical boundaries of wards for the upcoming elections of the Manchar
Nagarpanchayat have been published in the Government of Maharashtra Gazette, Extraordinary Part
One-A – Pune Divisional Supplement, dated 29th September 2025.
And whereas, as per the orders of the Hon. State Election Commission, Order No. RANIA/NP-
2025/Pr.No.10/Ka-6, dated 03/10/2025, and Order No. RANIA/NP-2025/Pr.No.11/Ka-6, dated
03/10/2025, the powers to publish a draft notification for reservation draw have been delegated.
Accordingly, as per the schedule mentioned in the said orders, the reservation draw for the wards of
the Manchar Nagarpanchayat was conducted and published on 08th October 2025.
Therefore, I, Jitendra Dudi, District Collector, Pune, in exercise of the powers vested in me
under the above-mentioned orders of the Hon. State Election Commission and as per the order of the
Urban Development Department dated 10/06/2025, hereby publish the draft proposal of reservation for
the upcoming general elections in the election wards of the Manchar Nagarpanchayat, as notified in
the Government of Maharashtra Gazette, Extraordinary Part One-A – Pune Divisional Supplement,
dated 29th September 2025.
In accordance with this notification, if citizens have any objections or suggestions, they should
submit them to the Chief Officer, Manchar Nagarpanchayat on or before 14th October 2025.
Objections or suggestions received after this date will not be considered.

GADHINGLAJ MUNICIPAL COUNCIL, GADHINGLAJ
No. Desk11/NP/sank.7/ElectionSR/137/2025.--Hon. State Election Commission Order :
Order No. RANIA/NP-2025/Pr.No.10/Ka-6, dated 03/10/2025, and Order No. RANIA/NP-
2025/Pr.No.11/Ka-6, dated 03/10/2025.
Whereas, as per the schedule issued by the Urban Development Department dated
10/06/2025, the final geographical boundaries of wards for the upcoming elections of the
Gadhinglaj Municipal Council have been published in the Government of Maharashtra Gazette,
Extraordinary Part One-A – Pune Divisional Supplement, dated 29th September 2025.
And whereas, as per the orders of the Hon. State Election Commission, Order No.
RANIA/NP-2025/Pr.No.10/Ka-6, dated 03/10/2025, and Order No. RANIA/NP-2025/Pr.No.11/Ka-
6, dated 03/10/2025, the powers to publish a draft notification for reservation draw have been
delegated. Accordingly, as per the schedule mentioned in the said orders, the reservation draw
for the wards of the Gadhinglaj Municipal Council was conducted and published on 08th
October 2025.
Therefore, I, Amol Yedage, District Collector, kolhapur, in exercise of the powers vested
in me under the above-mentioned orders of the Hon. State Election Commission and as per the
order of the Urban Development Department dated 10/06/2025, hereby publish the draft proposal
of reservation for the upcoming general elections in the election wards of the Gadhinglaj
Municipal Council as notified in the Government of Maharashtra Gazette, Extraordinary Part
One-A – Pune Divisional Supplement, dated 29th September 2025.
In accordance with this notification, if citizens have any objections or suggestions, they
should submit them to the Chief Officer Gadhinglaj Municipal Council on or before 14th October
2025. Objections or suggestions received after this date will not be considered.

HUPARI MUNICIPAL COUNCIL, HUPARI
No. Desk11/NP/sank.7/ElectionSR/131/2025.--Hon. State Election Commission Order :
Order No. RANIA/NP-2025/Pr.No.10/Ka-6, dated 03/10/2025, and Order No. RANIA/NP-
2025/Pr.No.11/Ka-6, dated 03/10/2025.
Whereas, as per the schedule issued by the Urban Development Department dated 10/06/2025,
the final geographical boundaries of wards for the upcoming elections of the Hupari Municipal Council
have been published in the Government of Maharashtra Gazette, Extraordinary Part One-A – Pune
Divisional Supplement, dated 29th September 2025.
And whereas, as per the orders of the Hon. State Election Commission, Order No. RANIA/NP-
2025/Pr.No.10/Ka-6, dated 03/10/2025, and Order No. RANIA/NP-2025/Pr.No.11/Ka-6, dated 03/10/2025,
the powers to publish a draft notification for reservation draw have been delegated. Accordingly, as per the
schedule mentioned in the said orders, the reservation draw for the wards of the Hupari Municipal Council
was conducted and published on 08th October 2025.
Therefore, I, Amol Yedage, District Collector, Kolhapur, in exercise of the powers vested in me
under the above-mentioned orders of the Hon. State Election Commission and as per the order of the
Urban Development Department dated 10/06/2025, hereby publish the draft proposal of reservation for the
upcoming general elections in the election wards of the Hupari Municipal Council, as notified in the
Government of Maharashtra Gazette, Extraordinary Part One-A – Pune Divisional Supplement, dated 29th
September 2025.
In accordance with this notification, if citizens have any objections or suggestions, they should
submit them to the Chief Officer, Hupari Municipal Council on or before 14th October 2025. Objections or
suggestions received after this date will not be considered.

SHIROL MUNICIPAL COUNCIL, SHIROL
No. Desk11/NP/sank.7/ElectionSR/139/2025.--Hon. State Election Commission Order :
Order No. RANIA/NP-2025/Pr.No.10/Ka-6, dated 03/10/2025, and Order No. RANIA/NP-
2025/Pr.No.11/Ka-6, dated 03/10/2025.
Whereas, as per the schedule issued by the Urban Development Department dated
10/06/2025, the final geographical boundaries of wards for the upcoming elections of the Shirol
Municipal Council / Nagarpanchayat have been published in the Government of Maharashtra
Gazette, Extraordinary Part One-A – Pune Divisional Supplement, dated 29th September 2025.
And whereas, as per the orders of the Hon. State Election Commission, Order No.
RANIA/NP-2025/Pr.No.10/Ka-6, dated 03/10/2025, and Order No. RANIA/NP-
2025/Pr.No.11/Ka-6, dated 03/10/2025, the powers to publish a draft notification for reservation
draw have been delegated. Accordingly, as per the schedule mentioned in the said orders, the
reservation draw for the wards of the ---Shirol Municipal Council was conducted and published
on 08th October 2025.
Therefore, I, Amol Yadage, District Collector, Kolhapur, in exercise of the powers vested
in me under the above-mentioned orders of the Hon. State Election Commission and as per the
order of the Urban Development Department dated 10/06/2025, hereby publish the draft proposal
of reservation for the upcoming general elections in the election wards of the Shirol Municipal
Council, Shirol as notified in the Government of Maharashtra Gazette, Extraordinary Part One-A
– Pune Divisional Supplement, dated 29th September 2025.
In accordance with this notification, if citizens have any objections or suggestions, they
should submit them to the Chief Officer, Shirol Municipal Council on or before 14th October
2025. Objections or suggestions received after this date will not be considered.

PANHALA HILL STATION MUNICIPAL COUNCIL, PANHALA
No. Desk11/NP/sank.7/ElectionSR/130/2025.--Hon. State Election Commission Order :
Order No. RANIA/NP-2025/Pr.No.10/Ka-6, dated 03/10/2025, and Order No. RANIA/NP-
2025/Pr.No.11/Ka-6, dated 03/10/2025.
Whereas, as per the schedule issued by the Urban Development Department dated
10/06/2025, the final geographical boundaries of wards for the upcoming elections of the
Panhala Hill Station Municipal Council,Panhala have been published in the Government of
Maharashtra Gazette, Extraordinary Part One-A – Pune Divisional Supplement, dated 29th
September 2025.
And whereas, as per the orders of the Hon. State Election Commission, Order No.
RANIA/NP-2025/Pr.No.10/Ka-6, dated 03/10/2025, and Order No. RANIA/NP-
2025/Pr.No.11/Ka-6, dated 03/10/2025, the powers to publish a draft notification for reservation
draw have been delegated. Accordingly, as per the schedule mentioned in the said orders, the
reservation draw for the wards of the Panhala Hill Station Municipal Municipal, Council
Panhala was conducted and published on 08th October 2025.
Therefore, I, Amol Yedage, District Collector, Kolhapur, in exercise of the powers
vested in me under the above-mentioned orders of the Hon. State Election Commission and as
per the order of the Urban Development Department dated 10/06/2025, hereby publish the draft
proposal of reservation for the upcoming general elections in the election wards of the Panhala
Hill Station Municipal, Panhala as notified in the Government of Maharashtra Gazette,
Extraordinary Part One-A Pune Divisional Supplement, dated 29th September 2025.
In accordance with this notification, if citizens have any objections or suggestions, they
should submit them to the Chief Officer, Panhala Hill Station Municipal Council, Panhala on or
before 14th October 2025. Objections or suggestions received after this date will not be
considered.

MALKAPUR MUNICIPAL COUNCIL, MALKAPUR
No. Desk11/NP/sank.7/ElectionSR/140/2025.--Hon. State Election Commission Order :
Order No. RANIA/NP-2025/Pr.No.10/Ka-6, dated 03/10/2025, and Order No. RANIA/NP-
2025/Pr.No.11/Ka-6, dated 03/10/2025.
Whereas, as per the schedule issued by the Urban Development Department dated
10/06/2025, the final geographical boundaries of wards for the upcoming elections of the
Malkapur Municipal Council have been published in the Government of Maharashtra Gazette,
Extraordinary Part One-A – Pune Divisional Supplement, dated 29th September 2025.
And whereas, as per the orders of the Hon. State Election Commission, Order No.
RANIA/NP-2025/Pr.No.10/Ka-6, dated 03/10/2025, and Order No. RANIA/NP-
2025/Pr.No.11/Ka-6, dated 03/10/2025, the powers to publish a draft notification for reservation
draw have been delegated. Accordingly, as per the schedule mentioned in the said orders, the
reservation draw for the wards of the Malkapur Municipal Council was conducted and published
on 08th October 2025.
Therefore, I, Amol Yedage, District Collector, Kolhapur, in exercise of the powers vested
in me under the above-mentioned orders of the Hon. State Election Commission and as per the
order of the Urban Development Department dated 10/06/2025, hereby publish the draft proposal
of reservation for the upcoming general elections in the election wards of the Malkapur Municipal
Council, as notified in the Government of Maharashtra Gazette, Extraordinary Part One-A – Pune
Divisional Supplement, dated 29th September 2025.
In accordance with this notification, if citizens have any objections or suggestions, they
should submit them to the Chief Officer, Malkapur Municipal Council on or before 14th October
2025. Objections or suggestions received after this date will not be considered.

JAYSINGPUR MUNICIPAL COUNCIL, JAYSINGPUR
No. Desk11/NP/sank.7/ElectionSR/138/2025.--Hon. State Election Commission Order :
Order No. RANIA/NP-2025/Pr.No.10/Ka-6, dated 03/10/2025, and Order No. RANIA/NP-
2025/Pr.No.11/Ka-6, dated 03/10/2025.
Whereas, as per the schedule issued by the Urban Development Department dated
10/06/2025, the final geographical boundaries of wards for the upcoming elections of the
Jaysingpur Municipal Council have been published in the Government of Maharashtra Gazette,
Extraordinary Part One-A – Pune Divisional Supplement, dated 29th September 2025.
And whereas, as per the orders of the Hon. State Election Commission, Order No.
RANIA/NP-2025/Pr.No.10/Ka-6, dated 03/10/2025, and Order No. RANIA/NP-2025/Pr.No.11/Ka-
6, dated 03/10/2025, the powers to publish a draft notification for reservation draw have been
delegated. Accordingly, as per the schedule mentioned in the said orders, the reservation draw
for the wards of the Jaysingpur Municipal was conducted and published on 08th October 2025.
Therefore, I, Amol Yedage, District Collector, Kolhapur in exercise of the powers vested
in me under the above-mentioned orders of the Hon. State Election Commission and as per the
order of the Urban Development Department dated 10/06/2025, hereby publish the draft proposal
of reservation for the upcoming general elections in the election wards of the Jaysingpur
Municipal Council as notified in the Government of Maharashtra Gazette, Extraordinary Part
One-A – Pune Divisional Supplement, dated 29th September 2025.
In accordance with this notification, if citizens have any objections or suggestions, they
should submit them to the Chief Officer, Jaysingpur
Municipal Council on or before 14th October 2025. Objections or suggestions received
after this date will not be considered.

KURUNDWAD MUNICIPAL COUNCIL, KURUNDWAD
No. Desk11/NP/sank.7/ElectionSR/136/2025.--Hon. State Election Commission Order :
Order No. RANIA/NP-2025/Pr.No.10/Ka-6, dated 03/10/2025, and Order No. RANIA/NP-
2025/Pr.No.11/Ka-6, dated 03/10/2025.
Whereas, as per the schedule issued by the Urban Development Department dated
10/06/2025, the final geographical boundaries of wards for the upcoming elections of the
Kurundwad Municipal Council have been published in the Government of Maharashtra Gazette,
Extraordinary Part One-A – Pune Divisional Supplement, dated 29th September 2025.
And whereas, as per the orders of the Hon. State Election Commission, Order
No.RANIA/NP-2025/Pr.No.10/Ka-6, dated 03/10/2025, and Order No.RANIA/NP-
2025/Pr.No.11/Ka-6, dated 03/10/2025, the powers to publish a draft notification for reservation
draw have been delegated. Accordingly, as per the schedule mentioned in the said orders, the
reservation draw for the wards of the Kurundwad Municipal Council was conducted and
published on 08th October 2025.
Therefore, I, Amol Yedage, District Collector, Kolhapur, in exercise of the powers vested
in me under the above-mentioned orders of the Hon. State Election Commission and as per the
order of the Urban Development Department dated 10/06/2025, hereby publish the draft proposal
of reservation for the upcoming general elections in the election wards of the Kurundwad
Municipal Council, as notified in the Government of Maharashtra Gazette, Extraordinary Part
One-A – Pune Divisional Supplement, dated 29th September 2025.
In accordance with this notification, if citizens have any objections or suggestions, they
should submit them to the Chief Officer, Kurundwad Municipal Council on or before 14th October
2025. Objections or suggestions received after this date will not be considered.

No. Desk11/NP/sank.7/ElectionSR/132/2025.--Hon. State Election Commission Order :
Order No. RANIA/NP-2025/Pr.No.10/Ka-6, dated 03/10/2025, and Order No. RANIA/NP-
2025/Pr.No.11/Ka-6, dated 03/10/2025.
Whereas, as per the Scheduled issued by the Urban Development Department dated
10/06/2025, the final geographical boundaries of wards for the upcoming elections of the
Hatkanangale Nagarpanchayat have been published in the Government of Maharashtra Gazette,
Extraordinary Part One-A – Pune Divisional Supplement, dated 29th September 2025.
And whereas, as per the orders of the Hon. State Election Commission, Order No.
RANIA/NP-2025/Pr.No.10B/Ka-6, dated 03/10/2025, and Order No. RANIA/NP-
2025/Pr.No.11/Ka-6, dated 03/10/2025, the powers to publish a draft notification for reservation
draw have been delegated. Accordingly, as per the Scheduled mentioned in the said orders, the
reservation draw for the wards of the Hatkanangale Nagarpanchayat was conducted and
published on 08th October 2025.
Therefore, I, Amol Yedage, District Collector, Kolhapur, in exercise of the powers vested
in me under the above-mentioned orders of the Hon. State Election Commission and as per the
order of the Urban Development Department dated 10/06/2025, hereby publish the draft proposal
of reservation for the upcoming general elections in the election wards of the Hatkanangale
Nagarpanchayat, as notified in the Government of Maharashtra Gazette, Extraordinary Part One-
A – Pune Divisional Supplement, dated 29th September 2025.
In accordance with this notification, if citizens have any objections or suggestions, they
should submit them to the Chief Officer, Hatkanangale Nagarpanchayat on or before 14th
October 2025. Objections or suggestions received after this date will not be considered.

KARAD MUNICIPAL COUNCIL, KARAD
No. Sasha/NP/Ele.Kavi/58/2025.--Hon. State Election Commission Order: Order No. RANIA/NP-
2025/Pr. No.10/Ka-6, dated 03/10/2025 and Order No.RANIA/NP 2025/Pr. No.11/Ka-6, dated 03/10/2025.
Whereas, as per the schedule issued by the Urban Development Department dated 10/06/2025,
the final geographical boundaries of wards for the upcoming elections of the Karad Municipal Council have
been published in the Government of Maharashtra Gazette, Extraordinary Part One-A – Pune Divisional
Supplement, dated 29th September 2025.
And whereas, as per the orders of the Hon. State Election Commission, Order No. RANIA/NP-
2025/Pr.No.10/Ka-6, dated 03/10/2025, and Order No. RANIA/NP-2025/Pr.No.11/Ka-6, dated 03/10/2025,
the powers to publish a draft notification for reservation draw have been delegated. Accordingly, as per the
schedule mentioned in the said orders, the reservation draw for the wards of the Karad Municipal Council
was conducted and published on 08th October 2025.
Therefore, I, Santosh Patil, District Collector, Satara, in exercise of the powers vested in me under
the above-mentioned orders of the Hon. State Election Commission and as per the order of the Urban
Development Department dated 10/06/2025, hereby publish the draft proposal of reservation for the
upcoming general elections in the election wards of the Karad Municipal Council as notified in the
Government of Maharashtra Gazette, Extraordinary Part One-A – Pune Divisional Supplement, dated 29th
September 2025.
In accordance with this notification, if citizens have any objections or suggestions, they should
submit them to the Chief Officer, Karad Municipal Council on or before 14th October 2025. Objections or
suggestions received after this date will not be considered.

PHALTAN MUNICIPAL COUNCIL, PHALTAN
No. NP/Election/Karya-05/Kavi-59/2025.--Hon. State Election Commission Order: Order No.
RANIA/NP-2025/Pr. No.10/Ka-6, dated 03/10/2025 and Order No.RANIA/NP 2025/Pr. No.11/Ka-6, dated
03/10/2025.
Whereas, as per the schedule issued by the Urban Development Department dated 10/06/2025,
the final geographical boundaries of wards for the upcoming elections of the Phaltan Municipal Council
have been published in the Government of Maharashtra Gazette, Extraordinary Part One-A – Pune
Divisional Supplement, dated 29th September 2025.
And whereas, as per the orders of the Hon. State Election Commission, Order No. RANIA/NP-
2025/Pr.No.10/Ka-6, dated 03/10/2025, and Order No. RANIA/NP-2025/Pr.No.11/Ka-6, dated 03/10/2025,
the powers to publish a draft notification for reservation draw have been delegated. Accordingly, as per the
schedule mentioned in the said orders, the reservation draw for the wards of the Phaltan Municipal Council
was conducted and published on 08th October 2025.
Therefore, I, Santosh Patil, District Collector, Satara, in exercise of the powers vested in me under
the above-mentioned orders of the Hon. State Election Commission and as per the order of the Urban
Development Department dated 10/06/2025, hereby publish the draft proposal of reservation for the
upcoming general elections in the election wards of the Phaltan Municipal Council, as notified in the
Government of Maharashtra Gazette, Extraordinary Part One-A – Pune Divisional Supplement, dated 29th
September 2025.
In accordance with this notification, if citizens have any objections or suggestions, they should
submit them to the Chief Officer, Phaltan Municipal Council on or before 14th October 2025. Objections or
suggestions received after this date will not be considered.

been published in the Government of Maharashtra Gazette, Extraordinary Part One-A – Pune Divisional
Supplement, dated 29th September 2025.
And whereas, as per the orders of the Hon. State Election Commission, Order No. RANIA/NP-
2025/Pr.No.10/Ka-6, dated 03/10/2025, and Order No. RANIA/NP-2025/Pr.No.11/Ka-6, dated 03/10/2025,
the powers to publish a draft notification for reservation draw have been delegated. Accordingly, as per the
schedule mentioned in the said orders, the reservation draw for the wards of the Wai Municipal Council
was conducted and published on 08th October 2025.
Therefore, I, Santosh Patil, District Collector, Satara, in exercise of the powers vested in me under
the above-mentioned orders of the Hon. State Election Commission and as per the order of the Urban
Development Department dated 10/06/2025, hereby publish the draft proposal of reservation for the
upcoming general elections in the election wards of the Wai Municipal Council, as notified in the
Government of Maharashtra Gazette, Extraordinary Part One-A – Pune Divisional Supplement, dated 29th
September 2025.
In accordance with this notification, if citizens have any objections or suggestions, they should
submit them to the Chief Officer, Wai Municipal Council on or before 14th October 2025. Objections or
suggestions received after this date will not be considered.

RAHIMATPUR MUNICIPAL COUNCIL, RAHIMATPUR
No. SASHA/NP/Election Kavi /64/2025.--Hon. State Election Commission Order: Order No.
RANIA/NP-2025/Pr. No.10/Ka-6, dated 03/10/2025 and Order No.RANIA/NP 2025/Pr. No.11/Ka-6, dated
03/10/2025.
Whereas, as per the schedule issued by the Urban Development Department dated 10/06/2025,
the final geographical boundaries of wards for the upcoming elections of the Rahimatpur Municipal
Council have been published in the Government of Maharashtra Gazette, Extraordinary Part One-A –
Pune Divisional Supplement, dated 29th September 2025.
And whereas, as per the orders of the Hon. State Election Commission, Order No. RANIA/NP-
2025/Pr.No.10/Ka-6, dated 03/10/2025, and Order No. RANIA/NP-2025/Pr.No.11/Ka-6, dated 03/10/2025,
the powers to publish a draft notification for reservation draw have been delegated. Accordingly, as per the
schedule mentioned in the said orders, the reservation draw for the wards of the Rahimatpur Municipal
Council was conducted and published on 08th October 2025.
Therefore, I, Santosh Patil, District Collector, Satara, in exercise of the powers vested in me under
the above-mentioned orders of the Hon. State Election Commission and as per the order of the Urban
Development Department dated 10/06/2025, hereby publish the draft proposal of reservation for the
upcoming general elections in the election wards of thevRahimatpur Municipal Council, as notified in the
Government of Maharashtra Gazette, Extraordinary Part One-A – Pune Divisional Supplement, dated 29th
September 2025.
In accordance with this notification, if citizens have any objections or suggestions, they should
submit them to the Chief Officer,Rahimatpur Municipal Council on or before 14th October 2025. Objections
or suggestions received after this date will not be considered.

MEDHA NAGARPANCHAYAT, MEDHA
No. A.SHA.Letter No. Sasha/NP/Ele.Kavi/66/2025.--Hon. State Election Commission Order: Order
No. RANIA/NP-2025/Pr. No.10/Ka-6, dated 03/10/2025 and Order No.RANIA/NP 2025/Pr. No.11/Ka-6,
dated 03/10/2025.
Whereas, as per the schedule issued by the Urban Development Department dated 10/06/2025,
the final geographical boundaries of wards for the upcoming elections of the Medha Nagarpanchayat have
been published in the Government of Maharashtra Gazette, Extraordinary Part One-A – Pune Divisional
Supplement, dated 29th September 2025.
And whereas, as per the orders of the Hon. State Election Commission, Order No. RANIA/NP-
2025/Pr.No.10/Ka-6, dated 03/10/2025, and Order No. RANIA/NP-2025/Pr.No.11/Ka-6, dated 03/10/2025,
the powers to publish a draft notification for reservation draw have been delegated. Accordingly, as per the
schedule mentioned in the said orders, the reservation draw for the wards of the Medha Nagarpanchayat
was conducted and published on 08th October 2025.
Therefore, I, Santosh Patil, District Collector, Satara, in exercise of the powers vested in me under
the above-mentioned orders of the Hon. State Election Commission and as per the order of the Urban
Development Department dated 10/06/2025, hereby publish the draft proposal of reservation for the
upcoming general elections in the election wards of the Medha Nagarpanchayat, as notified in the
Government of Maharashtra Gazette, Extraordinary Part One-A – Pune Divisional Supplement, dated 29th
September 2025.
In accordance with this notification, if citizens have any objections or suggestions, they should
submit them to the Chief Officer, Medha Nagarpanchayat on or before 14th October 2025. Objections or
suggestions received after this date will not be considered.

ISLAMPUR MUNICIPAL COUNCIL, ISLAMPUR
No. NP/Election/Karya-05/Kavi-552/2025.----- Hon. State Election Commission Order : Order
No. RANIA/NP-2025/Pr.No.10/Ka-6, dated 03/10/2025, and Order No. RANIA/NP-2025/Pr.No.11/Ka-
6, dated 03/10/2025.
Whereas, as per the schedule issued by the Urban Development Department dated
10/06/2025, the final geographical boundaries of wards for the upcoming elections of the Islampur
Municipal Council have been published in the Government of Maharashtra Gazette, Extraordinary Part
One-A – Pune Divisional Supplement, Extraordinary No. 13, dated 29th September 2025.
And whereas, as per the orders of the Hon. State Election Commission, Order No. RANIA /NP-
2025 /Pr.No.10 /Ka - 6, dated - 03/10/2025, and Order No. RANIA /NP-2025 /Pr.No.11 /Ka - 6, dated -
03/10/2025, the powers to publish a draft notification for reservation draw have been delegated.
Accordingly, as per the schedule mentioned in the said orders, the reservation draw for the wards of
the Islampur Municipal Council was conducted and published on 08th October 2025.
Therefore, I, Ashok Kakade, District Collector, Sangli, in exercise of the powers vested in me
under the above-mentioned orders of the Hon. State Election Commission and as per the order of the
Urban Development Department dated 10/06/2025, hereby publish the draft proposal of reservation for
the upcoming general elections in the election wards of the Islampur Municipal Council, as notified in
the Government of Maharashtra Gazette, Extraordinary Part One-A – Pune Divisional Supplement,
Extraordinary No. 13, dated 29th September 2025.
In accordance with this notification, if citizens have any objections or suggestions, they should
submit them to the Chief Officer, Islampur Municipal Council on or before 14th October 2025.
Objections or suggestions received after this date will not be considered.

VITA MUNICIPAL COUNCIL, VITA
No. NP/Election/Karya-05/Kavi-552/2025.---- Hon. State Election Commission Order : Order
No. RANIA/NP-2025/Pr.No.10/Ka-6, dated 3/10/2025 and Order No. RANIA/NP-2025/Pr.No.11/Ka-6,
dated 03/10/2025.
Whereas, as per the schedule issued by the Urban Development Department dated
10/06/2025, the final geographical boundaries of wards for the upcoming elections of the Vita Municipal
Council have been published in the Government of Maharashtra Gazette, Extraordinary Part One-A –
Pune Divisional Supplement, dated 29th September 2025.
nd whereas, as per the orders of the Hon. State Election Commission, Order No.RANIA/NP
2025/Pr.No.10/Ka-6, dated 03/10/2025, and Order No. RANIA/NP-2025/Pr.No.11/Ka-6, dated
03/10/2025, the powers to publish a draft notification for reservation draw have been delegated.
Accordingly, as per the schedule mentioned in the said orders, the reservation draw for the wards of
the Vita Municipal Council was conducted and published on 08th October 2025.
Therefore, I, Ashok Kakade, District Collector, Sangli, in exercise of the powers vested in me
under the above-mentioned orders of the Hon. State Election Commission and as per the order of the
Urban Development Department dated 10/06/2025, hereby publish the draft proposal of reservation for
the upcoming general elections in the election wards of the Vita Municipal Council , as notified in the
Government of Maharashtra Gazette, Extraordinary Part One-A – Pune Divisional Supplement, dated
29th September 2025.
In accordance with this notification, if citizens have any objections or suggestions, they should
submit them to the Chief Officer, Vita Municipal Council on or before 14th October 2025. Objections or
suggestions received after this date will not be considered.

ASHTA MUNICIPAL COUNCIL / NAGARPANCHAYATASHTA
No. NP/Election/Karya-05/Kavi-552/2025.----Hon. State Election Commission Order : Order No.
RANIA/NP-2025/Pr.No.10/Ka-6, dated 03/10/2025, and Order No. RANIA/NP-2025/Pr.No.11/Ka-6,
dated 03/10/2025.
Whereas, as per the schedule issued by the Urban Development Department dated
10/06/2025, the final geographical boundaries of wards for the upcoming elections of the Ashta
Municipal Council / Nagarpanchayat have been published in the Government of Maharashtra Gazette,
Extraordinary Part One-A – Pune Divisional Supplement, dated 29th September 2025.
And whereas, as per the orders of the Hon. State Election Commission, Order No. RANIA/NP-
2025/Pr.No.10/Ka-6, dated 03/10/2025, and Order No. RANIA/NP-2025/Pr.No.11/Ka-6, dated
03/10/2025, the powers to publish a draft notification for reservation draw have been delegated.
Accordingly, as per the schedule mentioned in the said orders, the reservation draw for the wards of
the Ashta Municipal Council / Nagarpanchayat was conducted and published on 08th October 2025.
Therefore, I, Ashok Kakade, District Collector, Sangli, in exercise of the powers vested in me
under the above-mentioned orders of the Hon. State Election Commission and as per the order of the
Urban Development Department dated 10/06/2025, hereby publish the draft proposal of reservation for
the upcoming general elections in the election wards of the Ashta Municipal Council / Nagarpanchayat,
as notified in the Government of Maharashtra Gazette, Extraordinary Part One-A – Pune Divisional
Supplement, dated 29th September 2025.
In accordance with this notification, if citizens have any objections or suggestions, they should
submit them to the Chief Officer, Ashta Municipal Council / Nagarpanchayat on or before 14th October
2025. Objections or suggestions received after this date will not be considered.

TASGAON MUNICIPAL COUNCIL, TASGAON
No. NP/Election/Karya-05/Kavi-552/2025.---- Hon. State Election Commission Order : Order
No. RANIA/NP-2025/Pr.No.10/Ka-6, dated 03/10/2025 and Order No. RANIA/NP-2025/Pr.No.11/Ka-
6, dated 03/10/2025.
Whereas, as per the schedule issued by the Urban Development Department dated
10/06/2025, the final geographical boundaries of wards for the upcoming elections of the Tasgaon
Municipal Council have been published in the Government of Maharashtra Gazette, Extraordinary Part
One-A – Pune Divisional Supplement, dated 29th September 2025.
And whereas, as per the orders of the Hon. State Election Commission, Order No. RANIA/NP-
2025/Pr.No.10/Ka-6, dated 03/10/2025, and Order No. RANIA/NP-2025/Pr.No.11/Ka-6, dated
03/10/2025, the powers to publish a draft notification for reservation draw have been delegated.
Accordingly, as per the schedule mentioned in the said orders, the reservation draw for the wards of
the Tasgaon Municipal Council was conducted and published on 08th October 2025.
Therefore, I, Ashok Kakade, District Collector, Sangli, in exercise of the powers vested in me
under the above-mentioned orders of the Hon. State Election Commission and as per the order of the
Urban Development Department dated 10/06/2025, hereby publish the draft proposal of reservation for
the upcoming general elections in the election wards of the Tasgaon Municipal Council, as notified in
the Government of Maharashtra Gazette, Extraordinary Part One-A – Pune Divisional Supplement,
dated 29th September 2025.
In accordance with this notification, if citizens have any objections or suggestions, they should
submit them to the Chief Officer, Tasgaon Municipal Council on or before 14th October 2025.
Objections or suggestions received after this date will not be considered.

JATH MUNICIPAL COUNCIL, JATH
No. NP/Election/Karya-05/Kavi- 552/2025.----- Hon. State Election Commission Order : Order
No. RANIA/NP-2025/Pr.No.10/Ka-6, dated 03/10/2025 and Order No. RANIA/NP-2025/Pr.No.11/Ka-6,
dated 03/10/2025.
Whereas, as per the schedule issued by the Urban Development Department dated
10/06/2025, the final geographical boundaries of wards for the upcoming elections of the Jath
Municipal Council have been published in the Government of Maharashtra Gazette, Extraordinary Part
One-A – Pune Divisional Supplement, dated 29th September 2025.
And whereas, as per the orders of the Hon. State Election Commission, Order No. RANIA/NP-
2025/Pr.No.10/Ka-6, dated 03/10/2025, and Order No.RANIA/NP-2025/Pr.No.11/Ka-6, dated
03/10/2025, the powers to publish a draft notification for reservation draw have been delegated.
Accordingly, as per the schedule mentioned in the said orders, the reservation draw for the wards of
the Jath Municipal Council was conducted and published on 08th October 2025.
Therefore, I, Ashok Kakade, District Collector, Sangli, in exercise of the powers vested in me
under the above-mentioned orders of the Hon. State Election Commission and as per the order of the
Urban Development Department dated 10/06/2025, hereby publish the draft proposal of reservation for
the upcoming general elections in the election wards of the Jath Municipal Council, as notified in the
Government of Maharashtra Gazette, Extraordinary Part One-A – Pune Divisional Supplement, dated
29th September 2025.
In accordance with this notification, if citizens have any objections or suggestions, they should
submit them to the Chief Officer, Jath Municipal Council on or before 14th October 2025. Objections or
suggestions received after this date will not be considered.

dated 03/10/2025.
Whereas, as per the schedule issued by the Urban Development Department dated
10/06/2025, the final geographical boundaries of wards for the upcoming elections of the Palus
Municipal Council / Nagarpanchayat have been published in the Government of Maharashtra Gazette,
Extraordinary Part One-A – Pune Divisional Supplement, dated 29th September 2025.
And whereas, as per the orders of the Hon. State Election Commission, Order No. RANIA/NP-
2025/Pr.No.10/Ka-6, dated 03/10/2025, and Order No. RANIA/NP-2025/Pr.No.11/Ka-6, dated
03/10/2025, the powers to publish a draft notification for reservation draw have been delegated.
Accordingly, as per the schedule mentioned in the said orders, the reservation draw for the wards of
the palus Municipal Council / Nagarpanchayat was conducted and published on 08th October 2025.
Therefore, I, Ashok Kakade, District Collector, Sangli, in exercise of the powers vested in me
under the above-mentioned orders of the Hon. State Election Commission and as per the order of the

the upcoming general elections in the election wards of the Palus Municipal Council / Nagarpanchayat,
as notified in the Government of Maharashtra Gazette, Extraordinary Part One-A – Pune Divisional

SHIRALA NAGARPANCHAYAT, SHIRALA
No. NP/Election/Karya-05/Kavi - 552/2025.-----Hon. State Election Commission Order : Order
No. RANIA/NP-2025/Pr.No.10/Ka-6, dated 03/10/2025 and Order No. RANIA/NP-2025/Pr.No.11/Ka-6,
dated 03/10/2025.
Whereas, as per the schedule issued by the Urban Development Department dated
10/06/2025, the final geographical boundaries of wards for the upcoming elections of the Shirala
Nagarpanchayat have been published in the Government of Maharashtra Gazette, Extraordinary Part
One-A – Pune Divisional Supplement, dated 29th September 2025.
And whereas, as per the orders of the Hon. State Election Commission, Order No. RANIA/NP-
2025/Pr.No.10/Ka-6, dated 03/10/2025 and Order No. RANIA/NP-2025/Pr.No.11/Ka-6, dated
03/10/2025, the powers to publish a draft notification for reservation draw have been delegated.
Accordingly, as per the schedule mentioned in the said orders, the reservation draw for the wards of
the Shirala Nagarpanchayat was conducted and published on 08th October 2025.
Therefore, I, Ashok Kakade, District Collector, Sangli, in exercise of the powers vested in me
under the above-mentioned orders of the Hon. State Election Commission and as per the order of the
Urban Development Department dated 10/06/2025, hereby publish the draft proposal of reservation for
the upcoming general elections in the election wards of the Shirala Nagarpanchayat, as notified in the
Government of Maharashtra Gazette, Extraordinary Part One-A – Pune Divisional Supplement, dated
29th September 2025.
In accordance with this notification, if citizens have any objections or suggestions, they should
submit them to the Chief Officer, Shirala Nagarpanchayat on or before 14th October 2025. Objections
or suggestions received after this date will not be considered.

BY COLLECTOR, SOLAPUR
BARSHI MUNICIPAL COUNCIL / NAGARPANCHAYAT, BARSHI

No. NAVI7/ KAVI. 857/2025.----- Hon. State Election Commission : Order No. RANIA/NP-
2025/Pr.No.10/Ka-6, dated 3/10/2025 and Letter No. RANIA/NP-2025/ Pr.No.11/Ka-6, dated
3/10/2025.
Whereas, as per the schedule issued by the Urban Development Department, dated
10/06/2025, the final geographical boundaries of wards for the upcoming elections of the Barshi
Municipal Council have been published in the Government of Maharashtra Gazette, Extraordinary Part
One-A – Pune Divisional Supplement, dated 29th September 2025.
And whereas, as per the orders of the Hon. State Election Commission, Order No. RANIA/NP-
2025/Pr.No.10/Ka-6, dated 03/10/2025, and Order No. RANIA/NP-2025/Pr.No.11/Ka-6, dated
03/10/2025 the powers to publish a Draft Notification for reservation draw have been delegated.
Accordingly, as per the schedule mentioned in the said orders, the reservation draw for the wards of
the Barshi Municipal Council was conducted and published on dated 8th October 2025.
Therefore, I, Kumar Ashirwad (I.A.S.), District Collector, Solapur, in exercise of the powers
vested in me under the above-mentioned orders of the Hon. State Election Commission and as per the
order of the Urban Development Department dated 10/06/2025, hereby publish the draft proposal of
reservation for the upcoming general elections in the election wards of the Barshi Municipal Council as
notified in the Government of Maharashtra Gazette, Extraordinary Part One-A – Pune Divisional
Supplement, dated 29th September 2025.
In accordance with this Notification, if citizens have any objections or suggestions, they should
submit them to the Chief Officer, Barshi Municipal Council on or before 14th October 2025. Objections
or suggestions received after this date will not be considered.

PANDHARPUR MUNICIPAL COUNCIL, PANDHARPUR
No. PNP/NAVI-7/KAVI-857/2025.-----Hon. State Election Commission : Order No. RANIA/NP-
2025/Pr.No.10/Ka-6, dated 03/10/2025 and Letter No. RANIA/NP-2025/ Pr.No.11/Ka-6, dated
03/10/2025.
Whereas, as per the schedule issued by the Urban Development Department dated
10/06/2025, the final geographical boundaries of wards for the upcoming elections of the Pandharpur
Municipal Council have been published in the Government of Maharashtra Gazette, Extraordinary Part
One-A – Pune Divisional Supplement, dated 29th September 2025.
And whereas, as per the orders of the Hon. State Election Commission, Order No. RANIA/NP-
2025/Pr.No.10/Ka-6, dated 03/10/2025, and Order No. RANIA/NP-2025/Pr.No.11/Ka-6, dated
03/10/2025, the powers to publish a draft Notification for reservation draw have been delegated.
Accordingly, as per the schedule mentioned in the said orders, the reservation draw for the wards of
the Pandharpur Municipal Council was conducted and published on dated 8th October 2025.
Therefore, I, Kumar Ashirwad, District Collector, Solapur, in exercise of the powers vested in
me under the above-mentioned orders of the Hon. State Election Commission and as per the order of
the Urban Development Department dated 10/06/2025, hereby publish the draft proposal of reservation
for the upcoming general elections in the election wards of the Pandharpur Municipal Council, as
notified in the Government of Maharashtra Gazette, Extraordinary Part One-A – Pune Divisional
Supplement, dated 29th September 2025.
In accordance with this notification, if citizens have any objections or suggestions, they should
submit them to the Chief Officer, Pandharpur Municipal Council on or before 14th October 2025.
Objections or suggestions received after this date will not be considered.

AKKALKOT MUNICIPAL COUNCIL, AKKALKOT
No. NAVI-7/KAVI-857/2025.---- Hon. State Election Commission : Order No. RANIA/NP-
2025/Pr.No.10/Ka-6, dated 03/10/2025 and Letter No. RANIA/NP-2025/Pr.No.11/Ka-6, dated
03/10/2025.
Whereas, as per the schedule issued by the Urban Development Department dated
10/06/2025, the final geographical boundaries of wards for the upcoming elections of the Akkalkot
Municipal Council have been published in the Government of Maharashtra Gazette, Extraordinary Part
One-A – Pune Divisional Supplement, dated 29th September 2025.
And whereas, as per the orders of the Hon. State Election Commission, Order No. RANIA/NP-
2025/Pr.No.10/Ka-6, dated 03/10/2025, and Order No. RANIA/NP-2025/Pr.No.11/Ka-6, dated
03/10/2025, the powers to publish a draft notification for reservation draw have been delegated.
Accordingly, as per the schedule mentioned in the said orders, the reservation draw for the wards of
the Akkalkot Municipal Council was conducted and published on 08th October 2025.
Therefore, I, Kumar Ashirwad (I.A.S.), District Collector, Solapur, in exercise of the powers
vested in me under the above-mentioned orders of the Hon. State Election Commission and as per the
order of the Urban Development Department dated 10/06/2025, hereby publish the draft proposal of
reservation for the upcoming general elections in the election wards of the Akkalkot Municipal Council,
as notified in the Government of Maharashtra Gazette, Extraordinary Part One-A – Pune Divisional

KURDUWADI MUNICIPAL COUNCIL, KURDUWADI
No. NAVI7/KAVI.-857/2025.---- Hon. State Election Commission : Order No. RANIA/NP-
2025/Pr.No.10/Ka-6, dated 03/10/2025 and Letter No. RANIA/NP-2025/Pr.No.11/Ka-6, dated
03/10/2025.
Whereas, as per the schedule issued by the Urban Development Department dated
10/06/2025, the final geographical boundaries of wards for the upcoming elections of the Kurduwadi
Municipal Council have been published in the Government of Maharashtra Gazette, Extraordinary Part
One-A - Pune Divisional Supplement, dated 29th September 2025.
And whereas, as per the orders of the Hon. State Election Commission, Order No. RANIA/NP-
2025/Pr.No.10/Ka-6, dated 03/10/2025 and Order No. RANIA/NP-2025/Pr.No.11 /Ka-6, dated
03/10/2025, the powers to publish a draft notification for reservation draw have been delegated.
Accordingly, as per the schedule mentioned in the said orders, the reservation draw for the wards of
the Kurduwadi Municipal Council at was conducted and published on 08th October 2025.
Therefore, I, Kumar Ashirwad (I.A.S.), District Collector, Solapur, in exercise of the powers
vested in me under the above-mentioned orders of the Hon. State Election Commission and as per the
order of the Urban Development Department dated 10/06/2025, hereby publish the draft proposal of
reservation for the upcoming general elections in the election wards of the Kurduwadi Municipal
Council as notified in the Government of Maharashtra Gazette, Extraordinary Part One-A - Pune
Divisional Supplement, dated 29th September 2025.
In accordance with this notification, if citizens have any objections or suggestions, they should
submit them to the Chief Officer, Kurduwadi Municipal Council on or before 14th October 2025.
Objections or suggestions received after this date will not be considered.

SANGOLA MUNICIPAL COUNCIL, SANGOLA
No. NAVI-7/KAVI.-857/2025.----- Hon. State Election Commission : Order No. RANIA/NP-
2025/Pr.No.10/Ka-6, dated 03/10/2025, and LetterNo. RANIA/NP-2025/Pr.No.11/Ka-6, dated
03/10/2025.
Whereas, as per the schedule issued by the Urban Development Department dated
10/06/2025, the final geographical boundaries of wards for the upcoming elections of the Sangola
Municipal Council have been published in the Government of Maharashtra Gazette, Extraordinary Part
One-A – Pune Divisional Supplement, dated 29th September 2025.
And whereas, as per the orders of the Hon. State Election Commission, Order No. RANIA/NP-
2025/Pr.No.10/Ka-6, dated 03/10/2025, and Order No. RANIA/NP-2025/Pr.No.11/Ka-6, dated
03/10/2025, the powers to publish a draft notification for reservation draw have been delegated.
Accordingly, as per the schedule mentioned in the said orders, the reservation draw for the wards of
the Sangola Municipal Council was conducted and published on 08th October 2025.
Therefore, I, Kumar Ashirwad (I.A.S.), District Collector, Solapur , in exercise of the powers
vested in me under the above-mentioned orders of the Hon. State Election Commission and as per the
order of the Urban Development Department dated 10/06/2025, hereby publish the draft proposal of
reservation for the upcoming general elections in the election wards of the Sangola Municipal Council
as notified in the Government of Maharashtra Gazette, Extraordinary Part One-A – Pune Divisional
Supplement, dated 29th September 2025.
In accordance with this notification, if citizens have any objections or suggestions, they should
submit them to the Chief Officer, Sangola Municipal Council on or before 14th October 2025.
Objections or suggestions received after this date will not be considered.

AKLUJ MUNICIPAL COUNCILAKLUJ
No: NAVI7/KAVI/857/2025.---Hon. State Election Commission Order No. RANIA/NP-2025/
Pr.No.10/Ka-6, dated 03/10/2025 and LetterNo. RANIA/NP-2025/Pr.No.11/Ka-6, dated 03/10/2025
Whereas, as per the schedule issued by the Urban Development Department dated
10/06/2025, the final geographical boundaries of wards for the upcoming elections of the Akluj
Municipal Council / Nagarpanchayat have been published in the Government of Maharashtra Gazette,
Extraordinary Part One-A – Pune Divisional Supplement, dated 29th September 2025.
And whereas, as per the orders of the Hon. State Election Commission, Order No. RANIA/NP-
2025/Pr.No.10/Ka-6, dated 03/10/2025 and Order No. RANIA/NP-2025/Pr.No.11/Ka-6, dated
03/10/2025, the powers to publish a draft notification for reservation draw have been delegated.
Accordingly, as per the schedule mentioned in the said orders, the reservation draw for the wards of
the Akluj Municipal Council was conducted and published on 08th October 2025.
Therefore, I, Kumar Ashirwad I.A.S.District Collector, Solapur , in exercise of the powers vested
in me under the above-mentioned orders of the Hon. State Election Commission and as per the order
of the Urban Development Department dated 10/06/2025, hereby publish the draft proposal of
reservation for the upcoming general elections in the election wards of the Akluj Municipal Council /
Nagarpanchayat as notified in the Government of Maharashtra Gazette, Extraordinary Part One-A –
Pune Divisional Supplement, dated 29th September 2025.
In accordance with this notification, if citizens have any objections or suggestions, they should
submit them to the Chief Officer, Akluj Municipal Council / Nagarpanchayat on or before 14th October
2025. Objections or suggestions received after this date will not be considered.

MANGLWEDHA MUNICIPAL COUNCILMANGLWEDHA
No: NAVI7/KAVI/857/2025.---Hon. State Election Commission Order No. RANIA/NP-2025/
Pr.No.10/Ka-6, dated 03/10/2025 and LetterNo. RANIA/NP-2025/Pr.No.11/Ka-6, dated 03/10/2025
Whereas, as per the schedule issued by the Urban Development Department dated
10/06/2025, the final geographical boundaries of wards for the upcoming elections of the Mangalwedha
Municipal Council have been published in the Government of Maharashtra Gazette, Extraordinary Part
One-A – Pune Divisional Supplement, dated 29th September 2025.
And whereas, as per the orders of the Hon. State Election Commission, Order No. RANIA/NP-
2025/Pr.No.10/Ka-6, dated 03/10/2025, and Order No. RANIA/NP-2025/Pr.No.11/Ka-6, dated
03/10/2025, the powers to publish a draft notification for reservation draw have been delegated.
Accordingly, as per the schedule mentioned in the said orders, the reservation draw for the wards of
the Mangalwedha Council was conducted and published on 08th October 2025.
Therefore, I, Kumar Ashirwad I.A.S.District Collector, Solapur , in exercise of the powers vested
in me under the above-mentioned orders of the Hon. State Election Commission and as per the order
of the Urban Development Department dated 10/06/2025, hereby publish the draft proposal of
reservation for the upcoming general elections in the election wards of the Mangalwedha Municipal
Council as notified in the Government of Maharashtra Gazette, Extraordinary Part One-A – Pune
Divisional Supplement, dated 29th September 2025.
In accordance with this notification, if citizens have any objections or suggestions, they should
submit them to the Chief Officer, Mangalwedha Municipal Council on or before 14th October 2025.
Objections or suggestions received after this date will not be considered.

notified in the Government of Maharashtra Gazette, Extraordinary Part One-A – Pune Divisional
Supplement, dated 29th September 2025.
In accordance with this notification, if citizens have any objections or suggestions, they should
submit them to the Chief Officer, Anagar Nagarpanchayat on or before 14th October 2025. Objections
or suggestions received after this date will not be considered.
